# Copy of All Inclusive Resort Ratings

**Source:** google-sheets

---

## READ ME

Please Make a Copy of this Google sheet from the File Menu (by selecting File > Make a Copy) |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | 
▶ |  | All Inclusive Resort Ratings

Welcome! I originally created this spreadsheet to help my family decide which resort was right for us. Once I realized how useful it was, I thought this tool could benefit other families and individuals alike with their travel plans. Hope it helps!

To effectively use this spreadsheet, make a copy and then you can use the filters found on the RESORTS tab. The original link prompts you to make a copy, but if you've gotten here through other means, select File > Make a Copy from the Menu or click on the link provided below. Any requests for Editor Access will be denied.

The original spreadsheet is updated often. I recommend getting a fresh copy if you haven't done so in a while. Click on the following link to make a new copy: |  | ▶ |  | About Average User Ratings

These ratings are an average of Trip Advisor, Google, Costco and Expedia. Trip Advisor ratings are calculated based on the number of Excellent reviews, the number of Very Good reviews, etc. Ratings are periodically updated.

Anything with a rating less than 85 is filtered out but feel free to turn that off if you want by clicking on checkbox above the Avg User Rating header on the RESORTS tab, or manually in the DATA tab).

If Value is important to you, sort by the Value Ratio column (by clicking on the checkbox above the Value Ratio heading on the RESORTS tab, or manually from highest to lowest on the DATA tab).


About Pricing

Use the information provided to help you compare resorts. Prices are representative of what you might expect to pay for a week's stay in March 2025.

Family
Prices are for one week, two adults and two kids in March 2025 or as close to it as I could find in the lowest priced room with a refundable rate booked direct with the resort.

Adult Only
Prices are for one week, two people in March 2025 or as close to it as I could find in the lowest priced room with a refundable rate booked direct with the resort.

Guaranteed Connecting Rooms
Guaranteed connecting rooms means you can choose this at the time of booking. It's not a request based on availability. |  | ▶ |  | Acknowledgements

I’d like to thank fellow reddit user @gothamfury who granted every item on my wish list and transformed the original version of this spreadsheet into what you see today. If you would like to show your appreciation for his hard work, please consider buying him a coffee below: |  |  |

 |  | Quick Tips

The filters on the RESORTS tab work best if you work from left to right, selecting only the filters you need.

You can reset filters by selecting their default value (in blue) or for any of the multi-select dropdowns, pressing delete once while the dropdown is selected. If you delete the actual dropdown, use CTRL-Z or COMMAND-Z to undo.

Some dropdowns have checkboxes below them that allow you to modify the filter to exclude or include the selected options.

Most filter headers have tooltip hints that appear when you hover over them.

The DATA sheet is also available if you prefer to filter and sort using the native filter feature Google Sheets offers. |  |  |  |  |  |  |  | @TheRealGuncho |  |  |
